# Certification coverage map

> **Generated 2026-10-04** from the "Certification coverage" table in every chapter, capstone and exam-prep README, joined with the repo's requirement checklist (1,935 requirement IDs from the four Claude exam guides, the Anthropic and OpenAI Academy course pages, the OpenAI developer tracks and the role competency matrix). Do not edit this file by hand: change a chapter's coverage table and regenerate (see [How to regenerate](#how-to-regenerate)).

## What this page is for

This repo prepares for the Claude certifications (CCAR-F first, then CCAR-P, with CCDV-F and CCAO-F as side goals), the OpenAI Academy pathway certificates, and the day-to-day job of an Applied AI Solution Architect. Each requirement has a stable ID. Every chapter lists the IDs it teaches in its own "Certification coverage" table. This page puts all of those tables together, so you can answer three questions quickly:

1. Which exam objectives are already taught, and where?
2. Which ones are still waiting for a planned chapter?
3. Where does the repo claim coverage it does not deliver (gaps)?

## How to read it

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Covered | The ID is listed in the coverage table of at least one built chapter, capstone or exam-prep page. **Primary** means a page lists it as its main home; **supporting only** means every listing says the page supports it and the main home is still to come (usually a planned chapter). |
| Planned | Not listed anywhere yet. The planned folder (from the curriculum plan) is not built. |
| Gap | The planned folder is built but does not list the ID, or a built page lists the ID but does not (fully) teach it. Every gap is listed in [Open gaps](#open-gaps). |
| Out of scope | Listed by the CCAR-F guide as out of scope (see [CCAR-F prep: out of scope](anthropic-claude/exam-prep/ccar-f/README.md#out-of-scope-what-will-not-be-tested)). The detail table says where the repo still touches the topic for other exams or for the job. |

- **Where** links go to the section a chapter cites (`P` = primary, `S` = supporting). One link per page; open the chapter's own coverage table for every cited section, notebook cell and self-check question.
- **Covered %** = covered ÷ (items − out of scope).
- The CCAR-F prep page also maps all 30 task statements, the appendix lists and the four preparation exercises to chapters with blanket ranges ("Supporting, primary: the chapters"). Those blanket rows are a map, not teaching, so they are **not** counted here; each CCAR-F item counts only where a chapter or capstone lists it by ID.
- Academy lessons that have no learning content (welcome pages, course surveys, Academy quizzes) count as covered when a chapter acknowledges them; you complete them on the Academy site.
- Anthropic Academy lesson-level items are summarized per course and chapter; course and module learning outcomes are listed one by one.

## Audit notes

- All requirement IDs cited in chapter tables exist in the requirement checklist (0 unknown IDs). All in-page anchors cited in the tables resolve (0 broken).
- Table header uses `Depth` instead of `Depth (Primary/Supporting)`: [Claude 01](anthropic-claude/01-claude-api-fundamentals/README.md), [Claude 02](anthropic-claude/02-tool-use/README.md), [Claude 03](anthropic-claude/03-agent-sdk/README.md), [Claude 10](anthropic-claude/10-multi-agent-error-handling/README.md), [Claude 13](anthropic-claude/13-claude-code-builtin-tools/README.md). Content is the same.
- Coverage section not between "Self-check" and "Official documentation" (overview pages without those sections): [Prerequisites](00-prerequisites/README.md), [Applied track](applied-ai-architect/README.md), [Capstones index](applied-ai-architect/capstones/README.md).
- The CCAR-F prep out-of-scope table shows "—" for computer use, streaming and cloud-provider configuration, but the repo touches them (see the out-of-scope rows above).

## How to regenerate

The generator is kept outside the repo, in the maintainer's working folder next to the requirement checklists (`coverage/parse.py`, `coverage/status.py`, `coverage/build_coverage.py`). It:

1. Reads every `README.md` in the repo and parses the table under `## Certification coverage`, expanding lists, ranges (`A` – `B`) and sibling shorthand (`S1 (K1–K3)`).
2. Joins the IDs with the coverage plan (`req_id, source, title, planned_location`) and the curriculum plan (planned folder names).
3. Assigns a status per ID as described in [How to read it](#how-to-read-it), checks that every cited anchor exists, and flags rows whose cited section shares few keywords with the requirement for a manual read.
4. Writes this file.

Run `python3 parse.py && python3 status.py && python3 build_coverage.py` from that folder after changing any coverage table.
